50\t\tp.Settings.Build.Branch = p.Metadata.Repository.Branch\n    51\t\tp.Settings.Build.Ref = p.Metadata.Curr.Ref\n    52\t\tp.Settings.Daemon.Registry = p.Settings.Registry.Address\n    53\t\n 63\t\t\t\t\ttrue,\n    64\t\t\t\t)\n    65\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n    66\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cannot generate tags from %s, invalid semantic version: %w\", p.Settings.Build.Ref, err)\n 86\t//nolint:gocognit\n    87\tfunc (p *Plugin) Execute(ctx context.Context) error {\n    88\t\tvar err error\n    89\t\n    90\t\thomeDir := plugin_util.GetUserHomeDir()\n    91\t\tbatchCmd := make([]*plugin_exec.Cmd, 0)\n    92\t\n    93\t\t// start the Docker daemon server\n    94\t\t//nolint: nestif\n    95\t\tif !p.Settings.Daemon.Disabled {\n    96\t\t\t// If no custom DNS value set detecting IP address: %v\", err)\n   101\t\t\t\t}\n   102\t\n   103\t\t\t\tif ip != \"\" {\n   104\t\t\t\t\tlog.Debug().Msgf(\"discovered IP address: %v\", ip)\n   105\t\n   106\t\t\t\t\tcmd := 112\t\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"error setting daemon dns: %w\", err)\n   113\t\t\t\t\t}\n   114\t\t\t\t}\n   115\t\t\t}\n   116\t\n   117\t\t\tcmd := p.Settings.Daemon.Start()\n   118\t\t\tgo func() {\n   119\t\t\t\t_ = cmd.Run()\n   120\t\t\t}()\n   121\t\t}\n   122\t\n   123\t\t// poll the docker daemon until it is started. This ensures the daemon is\n   124\t\t// ready to accept connections before we proceed.\n   125\t\tfor i := 0; i < 15; i++ {\n   126\t\t\tcmd := docker.Info()\n   127\t\n   128\t\t\terr := cmd.Run()\n   129\t\t\tif err == nil {\n   130\t\t\t\tbreak\n   131\t\t\t}\n   132\t\n   133\t\t\ttime.Sleep(time.Second * 1)\n   134\t\t}\n   135\t\n   136\t\tif p.Settings.Registry.Config != \"\" {\n   137\t\t\tpath := filepath.Join(homeDir, \".docker\", \"config.json\")\n   138\t\t\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), strictFilePerm); err != nil {\n   139\t\t\t\treturn err\n   140\t\t\t}\n   141\t\n   142\t\t\tif err := WriteDockerConf(path, p.Settings.Registry.Config); err != nil {\n   143\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"error writing docker config: %w\", err)\n   144\t\t\t}\n   145\t\t}\n   146\t\n   147\t\tif p.Settings.Registry.Password != \"\" {\n   148\t\t\tif err := p.Settings.Registry.Login().Run(); err != nil {\n   149\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"error authenticating: %w\", err)\n   150\t\t\t}\n   151\t\t}\n   152\t\n   153\t\tbuildkitConf := p.Settings.BuildkitConfig\n   154\t\tif buildkitConf != \"\" {\n   155\t\t\tif p.Settings.Daemon.BuildkitConfigFile, err = plugin_file.WriteTmpFile(\"buildkit.toml\", buildkitConf); err != nil {\n   156\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"error writing buildkit config: %w\", err)\n   157\t\t\t}\n   158\t\n   159\t\t\tdefer os.Remove(p.Settings.Daemon.BuildkitConfigFile)\n   160\t\t}\n   161\t\n   162\t\tswitch {\n   163\t\tcase p.Settings.Registry.Password != \"\":\n   164\t\t\tlog.Info().Msgf(\"Detected registry credentials\")\n   165\t\tcase p.Settings.Registry.Config != \"\":\n   166\t\t\tlog.Info().Msgf(\"Detected registry credentials file\")\n   167\t\tdefault:\n   168\t\t\tlog.Info().Msgf(\"Registry credentials or Docker config not provided. Guest mode enabled.\")\n   169\t\t}\n   170\t\n   171\t\tp.Settings.Build.AddProxyBuildArgs()\n   172\t\n   173\t\tbf := backoff.NewExponentialBackOff()\n   174\t\tbf.InitialInterval = 180\t\n   181\t\tbfn := func(err error, delay time.Duration) {\n   182\t\t\tlog.Error().Msgf(\"failed to run docker version command: %v: retry in %s\", err, delay.Truncate(time.Second))\n   183\t\t}\n 189\t\tif err != nil {\n   190\t\t\treturn err\n   191\t\t}\n   192\t\n   193\t\tbatchCmd = append(batchCmd, docker.Info())\n   194\t\tbatchCmd = append(batchCmd, p.Settings.Daemon.CreateBuilder())\n 207\t\n   208\t\treturn nil\n   209\t}\n   210\t\n   211\tfunc (p *Plugin) FlagsFromContext() error {\n   212\t\tcacheFrom, ok := p.Context.Generic(\"cache-from\").(*plugin_types.StringSliceFlag)\n
